# VOLUME 09 BATCH 0004, CHAPTERS 0431 TO 0440 — THE PEOPLE AT FOUR HUNDRED AND FORTY CHAPTERS, AND WHAT EACH OF THEM IS STILL REFUSED

*Appended downward by the phase that took `workspace/volume-09/batch-0004/PROMPT.md`. Nothing above this point is rewritten and no status about any person above is changed. **No want was resolved, no refusal was lifted, no name was printed, no undertaking was exercised or retired, and nobody was thanked, forgiven or sent for. The count of things anybody has asked out loud in this matter is seven and did not move, and this batch drafted five real askings and removed all five.**

**Marek Kest, thirty-eight, the protagonist.** Three chapters and in person in two of the three, want visible in one. **0433, absent, with the cost named in the room he is not in; 0434, in person and the point of view, and the only decision he takes in the batch is not asking; 0440, in person, present, and says nothing at all.** `grep -l 'Kest'` returns 0434 and `grep -l 'thirty-eight'` returns 0433 and 0434, so **the union is two and the floor is three**, and the third is the chapter where he is in the room carrying neither string. **He holds nothing at the end of the batch that he held at the start: no office, no fund, no list, no leave, no certification, no stage, and no second copy of anything.** **No piece of paper was put on his table in 0434 and the reason is on the page: a paper with nothing printed on it would have gone there and been found by whoever takes the room next, and there is a man in this matter he would want it to go to and he does not know where he is, and he is not going to be the one who makes him findable.** The four feet of wall is still bare, the nail is still in it and still crooked, nothing has ever hung on it and nothing is going to this week. **His reason for not reaching a stage is paid a second time zero times. He does not exercise the thing he is able to do. He is not a carrier and nothing in these ten files made him one. The name at the end of the struck line is not in any mouth in any of the ten and the hand that struck it is not either.** In 0440 he comes up four flights with nothing in his hands, stands at the end of the boards, buys nothing, is not served, is not asked for anything and goes back down, **and he is not named in that chapter**, because a woman of twenty-four is the only person who saw him arrive and is not going to say so.

**Tamsin Rook, twenty-four, the point of view of 0440.** **Her undertaking is live, unretired and unexercised, and it has not been exercised on any day of the two years, and its date is not said out loud in any of the ten files and is not said out loud by this state record either.** She is in at twenty minutes to the fifth, as she has been every working day for about seventeen weeks, **and the twenty minutes are still twenty minutes and are not a decision and are not on anything and are not going on anything.** What seventeen weeks has done to her is not a thing she did: **she has stopped saying things**, and nobody has asked her for any of them and nobody is going to. In 0440 she says one true thing at the counter, *it has not got a column either*, in 0435, unbidden, to the boards, and she is not thanked for it and is not answered. **The decision about whether she takes her colleague's judgment is unmade, was not made in 0435 or 0440, and is not made here.** She is a pair of hands across a counter in 0431's week as a pair of hands and nothing else. She is **not** an organiser, **not** a clerk with a title, **not** a person who has been given a post, **not** a person somebody has finally asked, and **not** a person who has been made a decision about. No romance has begun and nothing in 0440 is a moment in a doorway.

**Marn Ottery, thirty-four, at the far end of the boards.** In 0435 and 0440. **In 0435 she will not put a form that came back into the box of about four hundred blanks, and the reason is on the page in a mouth: a box is a supply, a thing that has been filled in and brought back is not a supply, and a person who puts one in a box has done a thing on purpose in a room with two other people in it.** She will not open the drawer, will not tell the man from the yard what came back in the satchel, and gives the reason out loud — a man who says on that floor that he does not know is a man who has said a thing in a room, and there is no form for a woman to be entered as the one who did not know. She says in 0440 one true thing nobody put to her and is not thanked for it. **The fourth item on the wall board is still the one none of the three of them can account for and is still in her own hand and about four years old.** The box did not open and did not move, the drawer did not open, nothing was ruled, no heading was printed over anything, and no space under a printed heading was filled in by anybody in these ten files. She was not thanked in either chapter.

**The clerk of about twenty-nine, at the far end of two joined tables, the point of view of 0435.** **She said one thing in that room in about four years and she has not said a second, and in 0435 she had the thing ready since about the fourth hour and did not say it** — the thing being what she said about a sheet with no column in 0430. She wrote none of it down, and **that is the second of those in about eleven weeks — the seven this row carried was wrong, and the first time she wrote none of it down is 0424 and the second is 0435 — and there is no form in this empire in which the one who stayed quiet can be entered as the one who stayed quiet.** In 0440 she is at her table with a sheet squared and her pen down and she says nothing. **She is not a function and no chapter in this batch used her as one**, and the only thing she does in 0435 is not speak, which is a person doing a thing.

**The man of about sixty-one, the second hand since he was forty-two, who cannot read a paragraph.** The point of view of 0431 and in 0432. **He has been given, for the first time in about nineteen years, a thing that asks nothing at all: no name, no mark, no reading, no paragraph, no day, nothing to fill in, and no form to tell him what the space under the line was for.** A sheet came up that stair off a pallet, not square with the others, nothing printed on the top of it, about nine lines of writing on it in somebody else's hand and no space left at the foot of it for anything, and **he signed it where it lay in about the length of a breath and a half and put the pen back in the pot and did not look up, and his hand did not do anything different on it than it did on the first of that morning.** He does not know what is in it, was not told, did not ask, and cannot ask. He gave a **fourth** answer to the pen pot, and the first three are his own from closed Chapters 0396 and 0427 and none of the three was answered by anybody: *Nineteen years, and I never once knew a thing did not want something. Today one did not, and I found out it is the only work I have ever done that is not asking me for anything.* **He is not thanked, nobody is sent for him, nobody sent him, and the two numbers he carries are not printed, not confirmed, not denied and not in a book anywhere. The nine miles is walked zero times. He is neither of the two women in their boxes and is not one of the four who cannot make sense of a paragraph and never is, and 0431 says so about him in the same breath as the four men in a bay on the far side of that town.** In 0432 he tells the young man he heard him, is not going to ask him what is in his pocket, and has not asked anybody anything in that corridor in nineteen years — **and he is not made curious, which was the standing the card set and which holds.**

**The young man from the shipping floor, the point of view of 0432.** **He is in 0431 nowhere and in 0432 in person, and the sheet inside his coat is not the one that was signed in 0431, and the two men are never in one room in any of the ten files and one is not described with the other's noun.** He filled a sheet in at fourpence in the first week of the eighth month, folded it in three, buttoned it inside a coat and went down four flights, said two sentences to a room that gave him nothing to say them to, was not thanked and was not asked. **About seventeen weeks later he is still carrying it, has never taken it out, and has now said out loud why it does not go up the second hand's stair, and the reason is a price and not a rule and not a refusal**: a basket that goes down at the seventh hour has been in a room with a man in it, and a coat is the cheapest place a piece of paper can be in this empire, and he has got one and the man of about sixty-one has not. **He is not a carrier, he is not a villain, he has no plan, he is on no list, and nothing in 0432 made him one.** He reads the top of about six sheets a year by accident and none of the six has been spoken about in that building.

**The man of about twenty-six who is paid by the corner.** In 0431 and in no other chapter of this batch. **He brought the sheet up off a pallet without knowing what it was, because he is paid for a corner and not for a page, and he is right that he could not have carried that one knowingly.** He said two things: *More of them. That is what I am saying*, which is nine words and is not the nine words of Chapter 0425, and *I have signed nothing in two years that wanted nothing off me and I do not know what happens to a man when one does.* **He is not the young man from the shipping floor and is not merged with him and is in no room with him.** The disagreement inside closed Chapter 0396 about his gender is carried unrepaired on purpose and is not settled here, because a repair that picked would be a fourth figure where two are already unreconciled.

**Rennick Adley, thirty-eight, the Company's book-keeper, four hundred yards up the lane off the Slade.** The point of view of 0433, and **this is the chapter in which he is a man of thirty-eight and the age sweep finds him and not the protagonist, and the two men are in no chapter, no sentence, no room and no sweep together.** **The count of his four lines is nine and not eleven**, which is the figure `state/character-state.md` corrected and the figure the batch prompt got wrong; the wrong eleven is named and not deleted. He has wanted the rest of a sentence for about two years, nine words of it are in his own hand on a page, the rest has not arrived, **and he has not asked and is not going to and there is no form in this empire in which a book-keeper may put down that he would like a sentence finished.** The bill at Lowcross is named once in his chapter and is unpaid and nobody is liable and the figure on it is not printed. **The four hundred yards is not walked, a blank is not carried up it, and a carrier who can be found has stopped being cheap.** The cost of the protagonist's absence is named in his room and he does not know the man's name. **A clerk of about twenty-two who brings that week's returns up to his bench is the only other person in the chapter and she resists him twice**, and she is not named and is not on the rota.

**The man of about thirty-four with the fair hand.** In 0434 and in no other chapter of this batch, and **he is not named in 0434 either** — he is *a man of about thirty-four with a fair hand* and the page has never given him a name to print in a chapter. **He was in the rented room two streets back from the river road, and that room was his before it was the protagonist's, and neither of them knows that and neither is going to be told.** He came, he was let in, and neither of them said a word for about nine seconds. **He was given a form and not a blank**: a printed heading over a ruled space, the space clean, came through a letter slot about two years ago, not returned to anybody, out of his house for about six weeks of that two years, and **he has never known who took it out and put it back.** He held it the whole time he was in the room and did not put it down and did not offer it and it went out in the hand it came in. **The number in his head is not printed, was not confirmed and was not denied, and the chapter states that nobody confirmed it and nobody denied it. The four words were not given to him in a mouth**, and he has never said them to anybody, which is what Chapter 0414 established. **The volume does not say whose the asking was and neither of the two men in that room found out.** He knocks on nothing and nothing is knocked on.

**Halla Wray, fifty-one, a foreman in a box entered by a class.** In 0437. **She said out loud, once, on her way out of the shed, and nobody asked her for it, why a person in a box entered by a class cannot carry a piece of paper: a class can be re-entered by whoever fills in the next form, and a person who can be put back is a party, and a party is a document.** She has had it since the first week and has never been able to spend it and was not thanked for it. **Nobody asked her for the second half of the sentence she began in a doorway in a week that is gone and it has still not arrived**, the nine words are in a book-keeper's hand four hundred yards up the lane, and the four hundred yards was not walked and he is not coming down and she is not going up. **The form that put her where she is is not asked to be void by anybody and nobody has asked, and the two of seventeen are at the back of that shed and are not named and were not spoken to, and the lamp is not lit until about the seventh hour.**

**The man of about fifty-five with the chain.** The point of view of 0437. **He can read a paragraph and has always been able to read one, and 0437 says in its own text that the four men on the far side of that town who cannot get the sense of one are not in that shed and never have been and he is not one of them and is not going to be.** He has had six months of the four sentences he said in that shed in the second week of the fifth month, and he has not apologised and is not going to and sorry is a form and he has not got one. **Nobody asked him about anything, nobody thanked him, and the four hundred yards was not walked on his account and not on hers.**

**The clerk of about fifty-three, nineteen years in one room off the old river road, and the woman of about nineteen who keeps the minutes under her.** 0436 is the clerk's chapter and the woman of about nineteen resists her. **The card this batch was handed placed the clerk in a room with a rail about four feet off the floor and there is no rail in that room and there has never been one** — Chapter 0411 gives two tables that are not joined, a chair at each, a third chair against the wall, a shelf under a window about a foot wide with no pane in any part of it, and about four feet of wall beside that shelf. **The rail is at the far end of the four hundred yards of cold flags, which is a different room and is not entered.** **A second line went into the book of one-line weeks and it stayed in, and the clerk did not take the book away and did not put her hand flat on it and did not tell the woman of about nineteen what to do and did not thank her, and neither of them gave the other an order.** The woman of about nineteen said out loud that she is not going to be told what to do about it and is not going to be thanked for it, and that the not being thanked is the half of it she came in there for and has had since the first week. **The question on the shelf has her name at the foot of it and no full stop and is not printed in 0436 and is not printed anywhere, and its author is not going to be asked a second thing about it by anybody.** The window has no pane and has not had one for nineteen years. **The two women are not the clerk of about fifty-three who is one of the three the state layer keeps apart from Marek Kest and the woman who keeps a rented house's ground floor, and neither of those two is in this chapter and neither of them is in 0439.**

**Nell Kest, twenty, at the counting room at the back of a dye end about four hundred and thirty miles up this river.** The point of view of 0438. **Sixteen volumes, fifteen out on loan, and the sixteenth has her own name in capitals at the head of it and has not been opened since it was written and she did not open it.** A man from the front office came in with the cart list and said out loud that all sixteen are going up on the second day, on two carts, and the second one comes back empty, and **he said it because he was told it upstairs and he is not going to have her find out from a man with a cart, and she could not turn him back from it.** Fifteen are on a cart about four miles off. **The wage of nineteen days of a crew's money is held in a coat four miles off in a room she has not stood in since she was seventeen and she is not going to ask what it is held against. A notice with a printed ruled space on the back of it is in the same pocket and the space is clean and has been clean for about two months through the washes, and she did not look at it once while the cart was being loaded.** The rule a woman said to her once in a lane has not been given to a third woman. **Nobody paid her, nobody sent for her, nobody thanked her, nobody forgave her, a season is not a life, she is not going up this river this season, and the four hundred and thirty miles is walked zero times and is not walked by her either.** **She is named as the protagonist's sister nowhere in these ten files and no merge is made with anybody.**

**The woman who keeps the ground floor of a rented house.** The point of view of 0439, and **this chapter is about eleven weeks after the one in which she said a true thing out loud alone, not the week after.** **In eleven weeks nothing has happened and that is not a relief and it is the reason she has not been sleeping.** He came down at the fifth hour on the same day of every week since and put his hand out and she put the rent into it and he has not said one word that he did not have to say and is not going to be different at her. **She thought of three repairs and did none of them, and the resistance in this chapter is the reader and not a person: the thing she resists is her own repair.** Moving the tin would take about nine seconds. Stopping the rent has a form and she would not know where to put the money. Telling the next man after this one would make the house not a silence for another twenty years. **A sensible thing done on purpose is a thing a person can be shown doing, and she put her hand on the boiler for the length of a breath and did not move the tin, and the tin is where it has been for about nine years.** The rent went out of a hand into a hand. Nothing was written anywhere. **She is not the clerk of about fifty-three, has never been inside that building, does not know what is said in it, and has not been up those stairs in about twenty years and is not going to this week. Nobody thanked her, nobody sent for her, nobody forgave her and nothing was resolved.**

**The man in the felt apron, the woman of about twenty-six at the lock, the woman of about forty with a scuttle, the woman of about thirty-five next door, the woman of about thirty-five with a day-book, the woman of about twenty-two in her second season, the two people in their boxes, the courier and the man who comes in on the second of the two days** are in no chapter of this batch, and **every one of those absences is correct and each is named here so that a cold reader can tell an absence from an oversight.** The four hundred yards of cold flags is not walked and the rail at the far end of it had nothing on it and is not entered, **and the room with the rail is not the room the clerk of about fifty-three is in**, which is the correction this batch had to make to a card. **The woman of about forty with a scuttle is in fourteen of the four hundred closed files and Chapter 0362 is named for her, and this volume adds no room, no trade, no week and no history to her.** The two women in their boxes stay in them and the form that put them there is not asked to be void by anybody.

## What the floor is and how it was measured, and why a string is not an answer to it

**Three chapters, 0433, 0434 and 0440, in person in two of the three, want visible in one, and the floor for a movement is three, in person in two, want visible in one, and it is met exactly.** The two strings return one file and two files and a union of two, and the answer is three, and the third is the chapter where he is in the room carrying neither string. **The sentence in each, printed because a count is not an answer to it:** 0433, *"There is a man in this city who has nothing behind him at all... and the price is that a person in that condition can be handed a piece of paper and there is nobody to hand it to"*; 0434, *"Marek Kest. Thirty-eight. A stage was never reached and none is coming, and there is nothing behind him this week"*; 0440, *"Then the stair, and a man came up it at about the sixth hour and he had nothing in his hands."* **The blind spot of the two sweeps is the standing Volume 08's sweeps were wrong about in seven files, and it is not fixed by reading either: what fixes it is the sentence, and the sentence has to be in a mouth and not in a record.**

## The four debts, restated in full, and this batch discharges none of them

**One: an owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005, four volumes on. Two, three and four: owed second readings of Volume 05's Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 and of Volume 06's own Batch 0003, which is four and not three.** Plus the review gate, which has opened and produced findings on all seven Batch 0005 phases the commit history records and has fallen back to the writing agent every time it has been asked and has never once produced a review that could be certified independent, **and a gate that only ever falls back is not a gate that passed either.** And the open items that are not plot, with the sentence bound among them, which are **eighty-nine by the high-water mark, eighty-seven entries measured in the rolling ledger before the eighth enforcement and three in it now, and the sixty-three this project has been quoting is twenty-four short, which is item 90, and this batch neither corrected that figure in place nor repeated it as true.** `state/phase-ledger.json` is a controller file, no phase may edit it, and it is carried and not repaired. **The number of debts has not moved in eight volumes and the number of phases that have claimed to discharge one is zero, and a batch is not among them, and the third of those debts has a tenth instance now, and the number of instances is not a measure of the debt: nobody but the hand that wrote these ten chapters has read them.**

# THE REPAIR OF VOLUME 09 BATCH 0004 — THE CHARACTER STATE, CORRECTED IN PLACE

*Appended downward by the phase that took `logs/batch-0004.review.log`. Nothing above this point is rewritten. **No person changed status. Nobody was thanked, forgiven or sent for, and the count of things anybody has asked out loud in this matter is seven and did not move.**

## The two rows whose facts changed, and what changed in each

**The man of about twenty-six who is paid by the corner.** He said two things in 0431 and said them in the same words the rows above used to carry, and the first of them was wrong. **The state layer printed the first saying as nine words over a sentence of thirteen, and printed the second as twenty-two over a sentence of twenty-five, and in one place printed the second as both nine and twenty-two in the same sentence.** The repair changed the sentence and not the count, because the nine-word motif is inherited from 0425 and is load-bearing in four other chapters. **The first saying is now *More of them. That is what I am saying*, and it is nine words, and the second is twenty-five words, and neither is the nine words of 0425.** He is not thanked, he got no answer, he is not the young man from the shipping floor, he is in no room with him in any of the ten files, and the disagreement inside closed 0396 about his gender is carried unrepaired on purpose. **What did not change about him: he is paid for a corner and not for a page, he brought the sheet up without knowing what it was, and he was right that he could not have carried that one knowingly.**

**The clerk of about twenty-nine, at the far end of two joined tables.** She had the thing ready since the fourth hour in 0435 and did not say it. **The row above said this was the second such time in about seven weeks and it is about eleven**, because in 0430 she spoke and the thing being counted is her writing none of it down, and the first time she did that is 0424. She is not a function and no chapter in this batch used her as one; the only thing she does in 0435 is not speak, which is a person doing a thing.

## The man in 0440, who is now identifiable and was not

**The batch left a man at the end of 0440's boards whom the chapter gave no means of identifying, and the state layer counted him as the protagonist in person, and the two men this project most often merges are kept apart by exactly the kind of detail that was missing.** 0433 is Rennick Adley, thirty-eight, a book-keeper four hundred yards up a lane off the Slade; the man in 0440 is thirty-eight years earlier a different man, and a reader with the volume in his hand could not have told which was which, because nothing in that file said. **The repair gave the man in 0440 the detail that makes him the protagonist by reading and not by a string: it is the third time in his life he has come up those four flights, and the stair has no door at the foot of it, and he has never knocked on that stair.** That is the man of 0407, who came up for the second time in his life and never knocked, and 0413 has Marn Ottery naming him at that counter outright in her own mouth. **He is still not named in 0440. He still bought nothing, was not served, asked for nothing, and went back down four flights, and Tamsin Rook is still the only person who saw him arrive and is still not going to say so, and the decision about whether she takes her colleague's judgment is still unmade.** The floor is met by reading, which is what the outline requires and what a string cannot do, and the strings are unchanged and still return two files against an answer of three.

## The characters this repair did not touch, and why that is a finding

**Nobody in this batch gained a post, a name, an age, a room, a want or a resolution, and the five sentences this repair wrote added no person to any chapter and moved no one out of any room.** That is the standing: a repair fixes a defect and does not develop the book. **The three characters the review's findings touch are all people the volume keeps unnamed on purpose — the man of about twenty-six who is paid by the corner, the man of about thirty-four with a fair hand, and the man who came up that stair — and the finding is that a state record can name a person a chapter does not, and that this project counts such a record as proof of a floor, which is the outline's own warning reproduced inside a batch that quoted the warning.** The cast file and the roster are unchanged, and no row of the volume's roster was added, removed or re-ordered by this repair.

---

*Appended downward by the phase that took `workspace/volume-09/batch-0005/PROMPT.md`, Volume 09 Batch 0005, Chapters 0441 to 0450, Movement Five, the climax. Nothing above this point is rewritten and no row of the Volume 09 roster above is added, removed or re-ordered. **The floor was verified by reading and not by a string, and the chapter numbers and the sentence that puts him in the room are printed in the batch summary rather than here.** Nobody in this batch gained an office, a fund, a list, a leave, a post, a commission, a warrant or a heading over his name, and no want moved except the two that were already moving.*

**Marek Kest, thirty-eight.** On the page in three of this movement's ten chapters, all three in person: **0447, 0448 and 0450.** He gains a piece of paper with nothing printed on it, which is the volume's smallest completed act and is a putting, not a reaching. **He does not fill it in, he does not exercise the thing he is able to exercise, he reaches no stage, and the reason he gave for not reaching a stage, which is in about nine words on a page and has been given once, is not paid a second time in this movement and is referred to nowhere.** In 0448 his want is visible and is not acted on: the folded leaf has been in the inside pocket of that coat for about two years, a line was put through what stood at the foot of it by a hand that was not his mother's, the claim that would have gone with it went with the line, and **the name is not printed in any chapter of this volume and the name of the hand that struck the line is not printed either.** The space at the foot of a sheet is the exact width of a name and he knows it and nothing is written in the space. **The name of the fair-handed man's number in his head is not confirmed and is not denied and was not mentioned in a mouth, and the figure at the lock end of the cold passage is not printed and was not handed on, and the eighth asking is his and is nobody's yet and this movement did not make it anybody's.** He was given a second copy of nothing. He was thanked by nobody and sent for by nobody.

**Tamsin Rook, twenty-four, at the fourth counter, two years, not a lawyer.** **The turn inside the turn, and it is hers, and it is a knowing and not a decision.** She worked out in the third week of the second month of the eighth named year what she has been standing ready for, which is a box of about four hundred pieces of paper that nobody can account for, and she worked out in the same week that a person with something behind her is not available, and **she said none of it out loud to anybody and is not going to.** She is not an organiser, not a clerk with a title, not a person somebody has finally asked, not a person who has been given a post, and nobody has given her a decision to make. **Her undertaking is live, unretired and unexercised, it has not been exercised on any day of the two years, its date is not said out loud in any chapter and is not said out loud in this state record, and it is not going to be exercised.** The twenty minutes before the fifth hour are still twenty minutes and are not a decision. She was thanked by nobody. **The decision about whether she takes her colleague's judgment on a thing like this is unmade and was not made in Chapter 0449 and is not made in this record.**

**Marn Ottery, thirty-four, at the other end of the same boards, eleven years, not a lawyer.** **She is the one who had the box moved, and she said the reason out loud before it was moved and was not thanked, and the sentence she used is a sentence a man said to her in a yard a long time ago: a clerk who moves a box is a clerk who has made a statement about it.** She said in 0441 that she is not going to say in that room that she wished the box out of there before the first week of the month, and there is no form anywhere for a woman to be entered as the one who wished a thing out of a room. She is thirty-four and not a lawyer and is never going to be one. **The drawer under the wall board is still shut and two of the four things in it have never been opened, and the fourth item on that board is still the one none of the three of them can account for, and nothing in this movement opened anything.** The box is not the supply and she said so on the page in 0410 and it is not contradicted here.

**The man of about twenty-six who is paid by the corner.** **He is a carrier in the page's own definition and this is the movement that makes him one in a body: he carried a box down four flights and about four miles, he put it down in a room on a second floor, and in 0447 he carried a piece of paper with nothing printed on it to the door of another room and put it on a table and could not say where he got it and did not knock.** He is about twenty-six and is paid for a corner and not for a page. **He is never in one room with the young man from the shipping floor in this movement, neither is described with the other's noun, and the young man is in the corridor in one chapter of it and this man is not in that chapter.** He is not a courier; the man from the yard with the satchel is a third person and was on a road with him for about a quarter of an hour and said one thing and went on. **He is going to keep the fact that he cannot be found, he said so out loud going down a stair, he has told nobody where the paper is now, and he has not been thanked and would refuse it.** He said in 0431 *More of them. That is what I am saying* and that is nine words and stands and is not paid a second time here.

**The man of about sixty-one, the second hand since he was forty-two, who cannot read a paragraph.** **New in this movement and it is the only new thing about him: in the last week of the seventh named year he signed a sheet that he had already signed the day before, because nobody told him and nobody could have, and he found out nothing and is not going to find out anything.** About nine hundred sheets a year go out of one building off the old river road and about nine of them a year are not what the person above him wrote and he signed all of them and is the only person alive who knows which and cannot write one down; both figures are on the page and both are unchanged. **The second mark is not a number and it is the first thing he has had to carry that is not one, and there is no pot on that sill for it.** He is not thanked. Nobody is sent for him and nobody sent him. **He is neither of the two women in their boxes and he is not one of the four who cannot make sense of a paragraph and never is, and the man of about fifty-five with the chain is not one of those four and never is, and neither of them was put in a bay with anybody in this movement.** The nine miles is walked zero times.

**Halla Wray, fifty-one, a foreman in a box entered by a class.** She is at the shed at the seventh hour with the lamp lit and a man at the open side who is not her, and **she stands at the open side and lets him look, having been told by the man of about fifty-five that it will cost her, and knowing what it costs.** She is not a carrier and the reason is already on the page in 0437 and is not said a second time here. **The two of seventeen are at the back of that shed and one of them stood in front of the board by the door and was not given the chance to say no, and the other did not look round, and neither of them was spoken to and neither is named and neither is going to be.** The second half of the sentence she began in a doorway has still not arrived and nobody asked her for it. The form on the board by the door is not asked to be void by anybody. The lamp is not lit before the seventh hour and no fire has ever been lit in that shed. She was thanked by nobody and asked for nothing.

**The man of about fifty-five with the chain.** He can read a paragraph and has always been able to read one, and **in this movement he says one thing unprompted, at the seventh hour, about what it costs to let a stranger look at the two at the back of that shed, and says out loud that it is not a fifth sentence and is not about the four.** Nobody asked him for it and nobody thanked him for it. He is not one of the four who cannot make sense of a paragraph and never is.

**The woman of twenty at the counting room at the back of a dye end.** **The sixteenth volume has her name in capitals at the head of it and is still unopened, in front of anybody or otherwise, and nobody in that building has ever asked to be there when it is.** The wage of nineteen days of a crew's money is held in a coat about four miles off in a room she has not stood in since she was seventeen, and the paper in that pocket with a printed ruled space on the back of it is still clean and she has not put a mark in it. **A man came up the lane about a book and did not get one, and she had the answer and did not give it, and the man from the front office refused to write a lot number and was right.** Nobody asked her anything, nobody paid her, nobody sent for her, nobody thanked her, nobody forgave her. A season is not a life and the four hundred and thirty miles is walked zero times. **She is not the woman of about twenty-six at the lock end of the cold flags and is not the woman of about forty with a scuttle, and no chapter of this movement compares a clean space on a paper with an empty space in a blank.**

**The woman who keeps the ground floor of a rented house.** **About twenty years at the bottom of a stranger's stairs and she has still never gone up them, and in this movement a man who is not the man whose rent it is goes up four steps in front of her and comes back down about a foot past her and neither of them says one word.** She refused to give him a name and gave the reason out loud: there is no name in that house and there never has been one. **She worked it out about nine years ago in about four minutes and has held it every day since — a person who has gone up a stair can be shown having gone up it — and she did not go up this time either.** The change in that house was made by somebody other than her and not at her asking and she cannot put it back. The tin is behind the boiler where she has kept it for about nine years and she has not counted it and is not going to. **She is not the clerk of about fifty-three and the two of them have never been under one roof, and nothing in this movement came out of that room and down to that house.**

**The man of about thirty-four with a fair hand.** He came to that room a second time in this movement, with nothing in his hands, and **said before he was asked that he is not going to be the man who takes the paper, and said he is not coming in to be thanked and not coming in to be asked anything.** The number in his head is not in the room and is not on anything and was neither confirmed nor denied. **Nobody asked him anything, the next asking is his, and this volume did not make it anybody's and neither did this movement.** He is not a carrier and cannot be one. Neither of the two men in that room found out whether the true thing he said was true.

**The characters this batch did not touch, and why that is a finding.** **The young man from the shipping floor, the clerk of about twenty-nine at the far end of the two joined tables, the man who comes in on the second of the two days, the man from the yard with the satchel, the man in the felt apron, the woman of about twenty-six at the lock end of the cold flags, the woman of about nineteen who keeps minutes, the clerk of about fifty-five, Rennick Adley, the woman of about thirty-five with the day-book, the girl in her second season, and the woman of about forty with a scuttle, are in none of the ten chapters or are in one of them without being developed.** **The two who are in a chapter of this movement and are not developed are the man from the yard with the satchel, who says one thing on a road and goes on, and the woman of about nineteen, who is not in a chapter of it at all.** Nothing was resolved and nobody was paid, thanked, forgiven, sent for, given an office, put on a list, or issued a form, and that is the arrangement and this movement holds it.

| --- | --- |**

| **Tamsin Rook's undertaking** | Live, unretired, unexercised, and its date is not said out loud in any chapter, is not said out loud by this repair, and is not said out loud by any row in this file. **The repair removed the one sentence in which Marn Ottery asked her for the thing underneath, and the undertaking is untouched by that and by everything else in the repair.** |

The same is true of the wrong-month references: the reading found three in Chapter 0410 and there is a fourth in Chapter 0407. **Both classes were cheap to find and neither was in any check this project owns, because every check here reads a string and an asking and a date-reference are both things a person did.** That is a standing instruction for Batch 0002 and it is not a plot thread and it closes nothing.

# VOLUME 09 BATCH 0003, CHAPTERS 0421 TO 0430 — THREADS

*Appended downward. Nothing above is rewritten, and no thread above is closed, moved, merged or resolved by this section.*

## The Volume 09 threads, and where each one stands after Chapter 0430

**A blank is filled in, and it is in a coat, and the count did not move.** OPEN, and it is the turn. 0423: a young man from the shipping floor buys one at fourpence, fills it in by hand, folds it, buttons it inside his coat and goes down four flights; nobody asked, nothing printed on it, fourpence into the ordinary place and the paper not. 0424: the woman of twenty-four who handed it over, a week later, in the point of view, says one word about it and it is *no*. 0430: the clerk of about twenty-nine describes it accurately in a room containing the woman who did it. **What is written on it is printed in no chapter and may not be printed in any of them. What it says is not on the page and is not going to be.**

**The sheet arrives at the foot of about four hundred sheets a year.** OPEN, and **this batch decided the question card 0427 left open, and decided it negatively, and the reason is in a mouth and not a record: a thing with no column does not travel to a floor with about four hundred sheets a year going up a stair past it, it travels to a coat, and the coat is on a back, and the back is the cheapest party in this matter.** So the second hand did not get it, and in nineteen years nothing has come up that stair wanting nothing off him, and he has said that once to a pen pot and is not going to say it again. **Movement Four has to open with it arriving, or has to say in a mouth why it arrives now and not then, and it may not arrive as a document and may not be turned into one.**

**A man of about thirty-four with the fair hand, and the four words.** OPEN, unchanged, and **not advanced by this batch beyond one sentence**: in 0429 he tells a woman of about thirty-five what a book with four headings does to a sheet with none, and that a column with a heading over it is a machine which cannot say no and cannot be hurt by what comes back. **The four words were not given in 0429 and may not be given. The figure in his head is not printed, not confirmed and not denied. Nobody asked him anything, he asked nobody anything, and he knocks on nothing and she knocks on nothing.** **The next asking is his and it is nobody's yet, and this batch did not make it anybody's and may not.**

**Whether Tamsin Rook trusts a colleague's judgment.** OPEN, and **put into a room in 0424 and refused in the room in the same breath, and still unmade.** Marn Ottery told her in 0418 that what she was standing ready for is nothing; in 0424 she told her that a person who comes in early for a reason has just watched the reason arrive and it did not come for her, and that what she is standing ready for is a man with a coat on going down a stair, and that is not the same trade. Tamsin Rook said one word and it was *no* and she is twenty minutes early again. **Her undertaking is live, unretired and unexercised and its date is not said out loud in any chapter and is not said out loud in this row either. Knowing is not a decision, being early is not a decision, and the twenty minutes are not on anything.**

**The thing Marn Ottery has been carrying for eleven years, and the fourth item on the board.** OPEN and unchanged. In 0430 she says out loud that she could have told the courier what was in the folded thing he put on her boards and did not, and gives the reason, **and the reason is not the one she gave in 0413 — in 0413 she refused it because a carrier is a person who can be shown what a thing is for, and in 0430 she has found out that a piece of paper with nothing printed on it cannot make anybody a party, so she would have been within her rights, and she did not do it because there is no form anywhere in which to be shown what a thing is for.** That is a movement in her and it costs nothing and resolves nothing. The box did not move, the drawer did not open, the fourth item is the one none of the three can account for.

**The four hundred yards of cold flags, the lock, and the rail.** OPEN, unchanged in substance, and **a lock was spent in this batch without being noticed by the batch that spent it**: Chapter 0416 already stands at two uses of the word *blank* at that end, and the standing is at most once in this volume's whole run of chapters at that end, so 0426 uses it **zero** times and says *a sheet with no heading printed over it* and *a piece of paper with nothing printed on it* throughout. **The next batch that writes a chapter at that end inherits a budget of zero, not a budget of one, and that is a number and not a feeling and it should be measured before the chapter is written and not after.** The man in the felt apron came down to the lock in 0426 and stood still and told her she is the person; she worked it out and said nothing. Nothing with a heading on it came down. The four hundred yards is not walked, the rail is not entered, the woman of about forty with a scuttle is in no chapter of this batch, which is correct.

**The second hand's own figures.** OPEN, untouched, and **not used in these ten chapters at all**: about nine hundred sheets a year and about nine of them a year that are not what the person above him wrote are printed in none of 0421 to 0430, are not confirmed, not denied and not put in a book, and he is asked nothing. **Movement Four may use both and the next volume question turns on the second of them.**

**The four hundred and thirty miles, the girl at the end of it, and the sixteen volumes.** OPEN, and **the road is not walked in these ten chapters and the girl is in no chapter of them.** The ground-floor woman said in 0417 that the road is not going to be walked on his account and that is not a kindness, and 0425 has her stopping a stranger on a step and saying she has a thing of her own and will not be handed a second one, and 0428 has her say out loud what the man under her stairs is, and neither of those mentions the road and neither closes anything.

**The question on the shelf under the window with no pane in it.** OPEN, untouched, carries no full stop, is printed out in no chapter of this batch, and its author is not asked a second time. **The shelf is not in any of the ten chapters, which is correct and is a standing and not an omission.**

**The nine words, and the fact that a true thing said twice in a street cannot be unsaid.** NEW, and OPEN, and added by this batch. In 0425 a young man says two true nine-word sentences about a piece of paper in a street, one to a shut door and one to a woman on a step, and is stopped twice, and **neither stop is unkind and neither stop takes it back out of the two people it was already said to.** Nobody thanked him. **This is not a plot thread and no chapter may make it one and no chapter may resolve it, and the next batch may not repeat it in a second street.**

**A tin behind a boiler.** NEW, and OPEN, and added by 0428. About nine years of a man's rent in coin in a ground floor, put there by a woman who knew what she was doing, and not moved when she found out that saying so out loud had cost her the only arrangement she had. **It is not a form, not a supply, not the box of blanks, not the coat four miles off, and it holds nothing written. Nobody knows it is there but her and it is not going to be shown to anybody, and the arrangement is broken and she is not going to repair it.**

## And what this batch did to no row above, gathered in one place, because a batch is where a lock gets dropped

No thread is closed, moved, merged or resolved. **The five premises in this batch's own prompt that did not hold the page are named in `state/current.md` and in `state/batch-summary.md` and deleted nowhere: the marker that exists, the checkpoint that does not, the sentence lifted from closed Chapter 0157 that the card required, and the two figures in the prompt's first paragraph that are true.** **The three corrections in the state layer are carried into every row above: the woman of about forty with a scuttle is in fourteen of the four hundred closed files and Chapter 0362 is named for her and she is in no chapter of this batch; the woman of about twenty-six is at the lock and the room with the rail is at the other end of the same four hundred yards and is not hers; `outline/volume-09.md` exists, is 111,429 bytes across 265 lines, is titled *The Blank*, and is the outline of record for Chapters 0401 to 0450.** None of the three is a plot thread, none closes anything, and item 43 is a debt and not a figure.

## And the four debts, restated in full, because a batch is where a debt is most likely to be quietly dropped

**One: an owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005, four volumes on. Two, three and four: owed second readings of Volume 05's Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 and of Volume 06's own Batch 0003, which is four and not three.** Plus the review gate, which has opened and produced findings on all seven Batch 0005 phases the commit history records and has fallen back to the writing agent every time it has been asked and has never once produced a review that could be certified independent, **and a gate that only ever falls back is not a gate that passed either.** And the open items that are not plot, with the sentence bound among them, which are **eighty-nine by the high-water mark, eighty-seven entries measured in the rolling ledger before the eighth enforcement and three in it now, and the sixty-three this project has been quoting is twenty-four short, which is item 90, and this batch neither corrected that figure in place nor repeated it as true.** `state/phase-ledger.json` is a controller file, no phase may edit it, and it is carried and not repaired. **The number of debts has not moved in eight volumes and the number of phases that have claimed to discharge one is zero, and a batch that inherited a ledger and no debt has been misinformed by somebody.**

# VOLUME 09 BATCH 0003 — THREADS, REPAIR PASS AFTER `logs/batch-0003.review.log`

*Appended downward. Nothing above is rewritten, and no thread above is closed, moved, merged or resolved by this section. This is a repair and it is not a review, not a self-audit, not a second reading, not a close and not a second batch.*

**No thread moved.** The repair edited five of the ten chapters of Batch 0003 — 0421, 0423, 0424, 0429 and 0430 — and every change was wording: two broken emphasis pairs, one canon sentence taken out of a mouth it does not belong to, three re-printed passages written out again, and two chapter openings moved off a shared inventory of the same room. **No thread was closed, opened, retimed or re-owned, no date moved, no canon figure moved, no person moved, and the count of things anybody has asked out loud in this matter is seven and did not move.**

**What the repair changed about a thread without changing the thread.** *The turn itself* — the young man from the shipping floor, fourpence, the sheet with nothing printed on it, about a minute and a half, folded in three, inside a coat, down four flights — is untouched, and what is written on it is still printed in no chapter of this volume and may not be printed in any of them. *The young man's own reasoning about a no* now reads in his speech rather than in Tamsin Rook's line from Volume 04, and it carries the same meaning and links the same way it did: a thing nobody is offered is a thing nobody can do, and putting it in a sheet with no column is not getting it down. *Marek Kest's argument to the woman with the day-book* is now his own and reaches the same place, and the woman still keeps her book, still rules no fifth column, still is not thanked, and the offer still stands unmade. *The counter at the top of four flights* is the same counter in the same four chapters; only its opening furniture moved, and the fourth item on the wall board and the year it was written are untouched.

**The findings the repair added are not plot threads and are recorded here as standing, closed as nothing.** A punctuation-blind twenty-word window cannot see a re-printed passage; a self-comparison adds windows and never removes them, and that is how a repair explained a pair of figures that were too low to be explained that way at all; a sweep run only against the closed page cannot see a batch repeating itself, and a volume's most-used room is where that happens first; a count of emphasis tokens returns an even number for a broken paragraph, so marker parity is not a marker check and a render is; and a figure in a state layer that does not reproduce against the page it describes is a premise, not a measurement.

**The four debts are unchanged and this repair discharges none of them.** An owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005, four volumes on. Three owed second readings, of Volume 05's Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 and of Volume 06's own Batch 0003. Plus the review gate, which has opened and produced findings on the four hundred closed chapters' batches and on every phase of this volume's own outline, handoff and three batches.

# VOLUME 09 BATCH 0004, CHAPTERS 0431 TO 0440 — THE THREAD ROWS

*Appended downward by the phase that took `workspace/volume-09/batch-0004/PROMPT.md`. Nothing above this point is rewritten. **Nothing in this batch is closed, resolved, thanked, forgiven or sent for. The count of things anybody has asked out loud in this matter is seven and did not move, and this batch drafted five real askings and removed all five and the number is not a batch that found none.**

## The plot threads this batch moved, and the state each is now in

**91. OPEN — a filled-in sheet with no column has reached the foot of about nine hundred sheets a year and was signed there.** It is in a basket in a building nine miles off with about two hundred ordinary ones. **Nobody in that building knows whose it is, nobody is going to be told, and nothing anywhere will bring it back out and be able to say which it was.** It is not a document, nobody made it one, and it is not in any book. It is not the young man from the shipping floor's sheet, which is still buttoned inside a coat and is the subject of Chapter 0432. **Not closed. Not a document. No form exists for a disagreement at the foot of a sheet and there is not going to be one.**

**92. OPEN — the man of about sixty-one has been given a thing that wants nothing off him for the first time in about nineteen years.** He does not know what is in it, was not told, did not ask and cannot ask. He has said a fourth thing to the pen pot and the fourth carries a reason the first three do not carry. **He is not thanked, nobody is sent for him, nobody sent him, and the two numbers he carries are not printed, not confirmed, not denied and not in a book anywhere.** The nine miles is walked zero times. **Not closed.**

**93. OPEN — the man of about twenty-six who is paid by the corner has said a true thing out loud that nobody answered and nobody thanked him for.** Two sayings: *More of them. That is what I am saying* and *I have signed nothing in two years that wanted nothing off me and I do not know what happens to a man when one does.* **He is not the young man from the shipping floor, is not merged with him, and is in no room with him in any of the ten files.** The disagreement inside closed Chapter 0396 about his gender is carried unrepaired on purpose and is not settled here. **Not closed.**

**94. OPEN — the young man from the shipping floor has carried a piece of paper in a coat for about a month and has now said out loud why it does not go up the second hand's stair.** The reason is a price and not a rule and not a refusal: a basket that goes down at the seventh hour has been in a room with a man in it, and a coat is the cheapest place a piece of paper can be in this empire. **Nobody asked him about it, nobody thanked him for it, and he is not a carrier and nothing in 0432 made him one.** **Not closed.**

**95. OPEN — the man of about thirty-four with a fair hand was given a form and not a blank, in a room that was his before it was the protagonist's, and neither man knows the room's history.** The form came through a letter slot about two years ago, was not returned to anybody, has been out of his house for about six weeks of that two years, and **he has never known who took it out and put it back.** He held it the whole time he was in the room and did not put it down. **The number in his head is not printed, not confirmed and not denied, and the chapter states that nobody confirmed it and nobody denied it. The four words were not given to him in a mouth. The blank is still the one thing in this empire that could put a question in front of him without anybody asking him anything, and he does not get one, and the volume does not say whose the asking is and this batch does not either.** **Not closed, and the next asking is nobody's yet.**

**96. OPEN — the protagonist did not ask, in 0434, and that was the only decision he took and it cost about four minutes.** A paper with nothing printed on it would have gone on the table and been found by whoever takes the room next, and there is a man in this matter he would want it to go to and he does not know where he is and **he is not going to be the one who makes him findable.** **No piece of paper went on that table. The four feet of wall is bare, the nail is in it and crooked, nothing has ever hung on it and nothing is going to this week. He holds nothing at the end of it that he held at the start: no office, no fund, no list, no leave, no certification, no stage, and no second copy of anything.** The erased name of his mother and the disputed family claim are unresolved, the name is not printed in any of the ten files and the name of the hand that struck the line is not. **His reason for not reaching a stage was paid a second time zero times. He is not a carrier. Not closed.**

**97. OPEN — a form came back to the counter with a clean ruled space and was not put in the box of about four hundred blanks.** The reason is on the page in a mouth: a box is a supply, a thing that has been filled in and brought back is not a supply, and a person who puts one in a box has done a thing on purpose in a room with two other people in it. It went down the four flights with nobody's name on it. **The box did not open and did not move and the drawer did not open and has been shut for eleven years and there are four things in it and two of them have never been looked at. Nothing was ruled, no heading was printed over anything, and no space under a printed heading was filled in by anybody in these ten files.** **Not closed.**

**98. OPEN — a second line is in the book of one-line weeks and it stayed in.** It says which of the four rooms off that passage it is that stands empty the way a room stands empty when the person in it is not a person in that building. The clerk of about fifty-three did not take the book away, did not put her hand flat on it, did not tell the woman of about nineteen what to do, and did not thank her. It has not been read again since and no third line has been added. **The question on the shelf under the window with no pane in it is still a question, carries no full stop, is not printed in 0436, and its author is not going to be asked a second thing about it by anybody.** The window has no pane and has not had one for nineteen years and nobody in that building has ever wanted one put in. **Not closed.**

**99. OPEN — Halla Wray has said out loud, once, why a person in a box entered by a class cannot carry a piece of paper, and was not thanked and nobody asked her for a fifth sentence.** The reason is that a class can be re-entered by whoever fills in the next form, and a person who can be put back is a party, and a party is a document. **She has had it since the first week and has never been able to spend it.** The second half of a sentence she began in a doorway has still not arrived and nobody asked her for it and the chapter is not about its arriving; the nine words are in a book-keeper's hand four hundred yards up that lane and have been for about two years, and he is not coming down for them and she is not going up. **The two of seventeen are at the back of that shed and are not named and were not spoken to. The form on the board by the door is not asked to be void by anybody. The four hundred yards is not walked. The man of about fifty-five with the chain can read a paragraph and has never been in a room with the four who cannot and never is.** **Not closed.**

**100. OPEN — all sixteen volumes are going up on the second day, on two carts, and one of them has not been opened.** Fifteen are on a cart about four miles off. The sixteenth has Nell Kest's own name in capitals at the head of it and is on a shelf behind her. The wage of nineteen days of a crew's money is in a coat four miles off in a room she has not stood in since she was seventeen. **A notice with a printed ruled space on the back of it is in the same pocket and the space is clean and has been clean for about two months and she did not look at it once while the cart was being loaded, and she is not going to ask what the wage is held against.** The rule a woman said to her once in a lane has not been given to a third woman. **Nobody paid her, nobody sent for her, nobody thanked her, nobody forgave her. A season is not a life. The four hundred and thirty miles is walked zero times and is not walked by her either.** **Not closed. The sixteen volumes are not resolved.**

**101. OPEN — the ground-floor arrangement is broken and has cost nothing yet, and the nothing is the cost.** About eleven weeks since she said a true thing out loud alone and cannot take it back. He came down every week since and put his hand out and nothing has happened and he is not going to be different at her. **She thought of three repairs and did none of them: moving the tin would take about nine seconds, stopping the rent has a form and she would not know where to put the money, and telling the next man after this one would make the house not a silence for another twenty years. A sensible thing done on purpose is a thing a person can be shown doing, and she is not going to spend twenty years on a boiler.** She put her hand on the boiler for the length of a breath and took it off. The tin is where it has been for about nine years. **She does not go up those stairs and has never gone up them. Nothing is written anywhere, no thread is closed, nobody thanked her, nobody sent for her.** **Not closed.**

**102. OPEN — Tamsin Rook has been twenty minutes early every working day for about seventeen weeks and has stopped saying things, and a man came up four flights in 0440 and bought nothing and went back down.** The twenty minutes are still twenty minutes and are not a decision and are not on anything and are not going on anything. **Her undertaking is live, unretired and unexercised and its date is not said out loud in any of the ten files and is not said out loud by this state record either.** **The decision about whether she takes her colleague's judgment is unmade, and it was not made in 0440 and is not made here.** **And he is identified in the chapter now, where as the batch left it he was not: 0440 says it is the third time in his life he has come up those four flights, and the stair has no door at the foot of it, and he has never knocked on that stair, which is the man of 0407, who came up for the second time in his life, and 0413 has a woman naming him at that counter outright. He is still not named in 0440, and the cheapest party in this matter is still a man who bought nothing and went back down, and the floor's in-person half of Movement Four is now met by reading and not by a claim.** She is the only person who saw him arrive and is not going to say so. The clerk of about twenty-nine said nothing and will not be asked for a second thing. **The box did not open, the drawer did not open, nothing was ruled, no fifth column exists, nobody was thanked, nothing was resolved. Not closed.**

## The findings this batch added are not plot threads and are recorded here as standing, closed as nothing

**A count of question marks returned zero on ten chapters that contained five real askings, all of which carried full stops, and all five were found by reading every quoted line in the batch for the class and not by running anything against it. Nothing this project owns can see an asking, and the instrument that finds one is the writer reading her own draft, and that is not a check and cannot be delegated.**

**A reconciliation between a total and its parts is a check of consistency and not of accuracy, and it passes on a wrong row whenever the error is common to the total and the part. The Batch 0003 per-file row, the batch total, the volume total and the manuscript total in the state layer are all wrong by the same thirty-five words, they all reconcile with each other, and the subtraction that appears to verify them is the one instrument that cannot detect that class of error. The only instrument that can is re-measuring the parts, and this batch did, with three methods that agree.**

**A classifier that sorts a window hit into formula or prose must be positional and not lexical, because a twenty-word window straddling the edge of a date line contains no date vocabulary, and the first classifier in this batch reported 239 of 250 windows as prose where the true figure is 0. And the same classifier's formula bucket knows one formula, being a date, and this volume has at least one other, and a classifier given a list of length one reports every other formula as prose forever.**

**A sweep run only against the closed page cannot see a batch repeating itself, and this batch's own ten files shared nineteen re-printed passages with the thirty earlier chapters of their own volume and two with each other, and all twenty-one were found only because the window was run against Volume 09's own files as well. A volume's most-used room is where a batch will repeat itself first, and the room that repeated most in these ten was the corridor, the shed and the ground floor, which are three of the six rooms on the outline's list.**

**A premise handed to a phase is not a fact, and this batch's prompt handed its writer six that did not hold the page and four that did, and the six were caught by opening the tree, the date lines, the state layer and the closed chapters, and not one of them was caught by reading a chapter.** The six are the phrase misidentified at `chapter-0407.md:29`, the rail in a room that has never had one, the eleventh time against a figure of nine, the eight weeks against a calendar that says seventeen, the week after against a calendar that says eleven, and the Batch 0003 per-file row that reproduces at no commit.

**And a state record that describes a number is a record about a measurement, and the way that fails is a row that is internally consistent and externally wrong, and it is a different failure from a state record that describes the repository and is wrong about the world. Three batches have now found one of each, and neither class is checked by anything this project owns.**

**The four debts are unchanged and this batch discharges none of them.** An owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005, four volumes on. Three owed second readings, of Volume 05's Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 and of Volume 06's own Batch 0003, which is four and not three. The review gate, which has opened and produced findings on all seven Batch 0005 phases the commit history records and has fallen back to the writing agent every time and has never once produced a review that could be certified independent, **and a gate that only ever falls back is not a gate that passed either.** And the open items that are not plot, with the sentence bound among them, which are eighty-nine by the high-water mark, eighty-seven entries measured in the rolling ledger before the eighth enforcement and three in it now, **and the sixty-three this project has been quoting is twenty-four short, which is item 90, and it is not corrected in place here and is not repeated as true.** `state/phase-ledger.json` is a controller file, no phase may edit it, it reads `phase-000-bootstrap` and `planned`, and it is carried and not repaired. **The number of debts has not moved in eight volumes and the number of phases that have claimed to discharge one is zero.**

# THE REPAIR OF VOLUME 09 BATCH 0004 — THE THREAD ROWS, CORRECTED IN PLACE

*Appended downward by the phase that took `logs/batch-0004.review.log`. Nothing above this point is rewritten. **No thread opened, closed, moved, merged or resolved. The count of things anybody has asked out loud in this matter is seven and did not move. The five rows this repair touched are 93, 97, 102 and the two elapsed figures inside 97 and 102, and it touched them because they carried a figure or an assertion that did not reproduce against the page.**

## The four corrections, and which of them is a lock

**One, and it is a lock. Thread 93's two sayings were re-counted and the state layer had both counts wrong.** It printed the first as nine words over a sentence of thirteen, and the second as twenty-two over a sentence of twenty-five. **The repair changed the sentence and not the count**, because the nine-word motif is inherited from 0425 and is load-bearing in four other chapters, and the count was the thing that was wrong. The first saying is now *More of them. That is what I am saying* and is nine words, and the second is twenty-five, and neither is a refrain. **The wrong text is named in `state/continuity.md`, in `state/batch-summary.md` and in `state/character-state.md` and is deleted nowhere.**

**Two, and it is a lock, and the state layer denied it. Two weekday names were in the batch and the section that reported the weekday sweep as clean was the false one.** *Tuesday* at the old `chapter-0432.md:33` and *Wednesday* at the old `chapter-0440.md:21` were the only two in forty files, and both are out of the page, and the sweep now returns zero in the ten and zero in the forty. **A thread does not have a weekday in it and no thread is closed by one, and what is added here is the standing and not a thread: a negative result printed in a state file is the one figure in this project's record that nothing re-measures, because a later phase reads a clean zero and inherits it as a fact about the page instead of as a result that expired when a different phase touched a file.**

**Three, and it is a lock, and the review is what found it. Thread 97's second time was printed as about seven weeks and is about eleven.** The clerk of about twenty-nine wrote none of it down first in 0424, the second week of the eighth month, and again in 0435, the first week of the eleventh, and that is eleven weeks. **The finding is in the referent and not in the arithmetic: in 0430 the clerk spoke, and the review named 0430 as the first instance, and the thing being counted is her writing none of it down, which 0430 does not have.** An elapsed figure is a claim about what an event is, and getting the interval right is worth nothing if the event is the wrong one.

**Four, and this is the finding the review did not make, and it is the same class. 0434 dated a counter visit four months ago and the calendar gives twenty-seven weeks, and 0413 calls the same visit six weeks.** It is about six months now. **Three elapsed figures in ten chapters, two found and one missed, and the class is the one this project already knows about in a different guise: a figure in a chapter is a claim about the calendar, the calendar is countable, and it was checked by reading in all three cases.**

## The one row that changed shape, and it changed because the page changed

**Thread 102. The batch left 0440 with a man in it and no way to tell who he was, and the floor's in-person half of Movement Four rested on a state record asserting an identification the chapter never made.** That is the outline's own standing — *a floor counted by chapter is met by a sentence in somebody else's mouth* — reproduced inside a batch whose own summary printed the sentence and called it proof. **The repair put the identification on the page and did not touch the thread's state: 0440 now says it is the third time in his life he has come up those four flights, and the stair has no door at the foot of it, and he has never knocked on that stair, which is the man of 0407, who came up for the second time in his life and never knocked, and 0413 has a woman naming him at that counter outright. He is still not named in 0440. He still bought nothing, was not served, asked for nothing, and went back down four flights, and the woman of twenty-four is still the only person who saw him arrive and is still not going to say so.** The thread is OPEN before the repair and OPEN after it, and what changed is that the claim in the state layer now has a page under it.

## The figures, re-measured after the last edit to any chapter, with the method named before each

Per file, `sed 's/[[:space:]]*$//' file | wc -w`, one file at a time and never with a glob: **1,478 / 1,226 / 1,427 / 1,540 / 1,263 / 1,114 / 1,181 / 1,084 / 1,179 / 1,273, the batch 12,765.** Volume 09 **56,865** across forty files. The manuscript **1,148,742** per-file sum and **1,148,739** by concatenation. The four hundred closed files **1,091,877**, unchanged, because this repair opened no closed chapter. **The figures before it, 1,482 / 1,227 / 1,427 / 1,539 / 1,263 / 1,114 / 1,181 / 1,084 / 1,179 / 1,220, the batch 12,716, Volume 09 56,816 and the manuscript 1,148,693, were correct for the manuscript as the batch left it, are named here and deleted nowhere, and the difference is exactly the five edited chapters.** 1,148,742 less 14,325 less 15,378 less 14,397 less 12,765 is 1,091,877 exactly, **and 1,148,693 less those same four less 12,716 is 1,091,877 exactly, and both reconcile, and this is the third batch in a row to find that a subtraction between a total and its parts cannot detect an error common to both.**

## The findings this repair added, which are not plot threads and are closed as nothing

**A lock written down as held has been checked by nobody, and two locks this project states in its own prompt were broken in the batch the prompt sent, and both were found by running a sweep and neither was found by reading the chapter.** A weekday is two words in a sentence and a reader will not stop on it; `grep -c -iE` finds it in a second.

**A stated count of words in a quoted sentence is a measurement, and it is the one measurement in this project that no instrument runs, because the sentence is quoted in a chapter and the count is in a state file and nothing joins them.** Nine claimed for thirteen, twenty-two for twenty-five, and nine words in the same state sentence for a twenty-five-word sentence. **The fix that was available and was not taken is to count it, and the count is four seconds of work that a chapter is not written with.**

**An elapsed figure names an event before it names an interval, and getting the interval right is worth nothing when the event is wrong.** The review found seven weeks where the calendar gives eleven and attributed both figures to 0430, and 0430 is the week the clerk spoke and not the week she wrote nothing down, so the review's own referent was wrong while its arithmetic was merely incomplete. **A finding that arrives with its own wrong premise is still a finding, and applying it without checking the premise would have written eleven words into a chapter in place of a sentence.**

## The four debts, unchanged, and this repair discharges none of them

**One: an owed review of Volume 04's Batch 0005, four volumes on. Two, three and four: owed second readings of Volume 05's Batches 0003, 0004 and 0005 and of Volume 06's own Batch 0003, which is four and not three.** Plus the review gate, which has opened and produced findings on all seven Batch 0005 phases the commit history records and has fallen back to the writing agent every time and has never once produced a review that could be certified independent, **and a gate that only ever falls back is not a gate that passed either.** And the open items that are not plot, with the sentence bound among them, which are **eighty-nine by the high-water mark, eighty-seven entries measured in the rolling ledger before the eighth enforcement and three in it now, and the sixty-three this project has been quoting is twenty-four short, which is item 90, and this repair neither corrected that figure in place nor repeated it as true.** `state/phase-ledger.json` is a controller file, no phase may edit it, it reads `phase-000-bootstrap` and `planned`, and it is carried and not repaired. **The number of debts has not moved in eight volumes and the number of phases that have claimed to discharge one is zero, and a repair is not among them, and the third of those debts has a tenth instance now: this one produced six findings and four of the six were a state record disagreeing with the page it describes, which is the same failure the gate exists to catch and which the gate found this time.**
